Boundaries of the finding

What remains uncertain

  • This simplified synthetic world omits real ground, drainage and measurement uncertainties.
  • Synthetic observations share this model’s assumptions. Reproducing them does not validate real-world forecasting. A changed model result is not an edit to the original observations.

This simplified model can demonstrate a mechanism or distinguish its listed scenarios. It does not independently establish a real historical cause, predict an emergency, or validate a real intervention.
